        \newbox\Wrappedcontinuationbox

        \newcommand*\Wrappedvisiblespace {\textcolor{red}{\textvisiblespace}}

        \newcommand*\Wrappedcontinuationindent {3ex }
        \newcommand*\Wrappedafterbreak {\kern\Wrappedcontinuationindent\copy\Wrappedcontinuationbox}
        % Take advantage of the already applied Pygments mark-up to insert
        % potential linebreaks for TeX processing.
        %        {, <, #, %, $, ' and ": go to next line.
        %        _, }, ^, &, >, - and ~: stay at end of broken line.
        % Use of \textquotesingle for straight quote.
        \newcommand*\Wrappedbreaksatspecials {%
            \def\PYGZus{\discretionary{\char`\_}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\_}}%
            \def\PYGZob{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\{}{\char`\{}}%
            \def\PYGZcb{\discretionary{\char`\}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\}}}%
            \def\PYGZca{\discretionary{\char`\^}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\^}}%
            \def\PYGZam{\discretionary{\char`\&}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\&}}%
            \def\PYGZlt{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\<}{\char`\<}}%
            \def\PYGZgt{\discretionary{\char`\>}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\>}}%
            \def\PYGZsh{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\#}{\char`\#}}%
            \def\PYGZpc{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\%}{\char`\%}}%
            \def\PYGZdl{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\$}{\char`\$}}%
            \def\PYGZhy{\discretionary{\char`\-}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\-}}%
            \def\PYGZsq{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\textquotesingle}{\textquotesingle}}%
            \def\PYGZdq{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\"}{\char`\"}}%
            \def\PYGZti{\discretionary{\char`\~}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\~}}%
        }
        % Some characters . , ; ? ! / are not pygmentized.
        % This macro makes them "active" and they will insert potential linebreaks
        \newcommand*\Wrappedbreaksatpunct {%
            \lccode`\~`\.\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\.}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\.}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\,\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\,}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\,}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\;\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\;}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\;}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\:\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\:}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\:}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\?\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\?}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\?}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\!\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\!}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\!}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\/\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\/}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\/}}}%
            \catcode`\.\active
            \catcode`\,\active
            \catcode`\;\active
            \catcode`\:\active
            \catcode`\?\active
            \catcode`\!\active
            \catcode`\/\active
            \lccode`\~`\~
        }

        \def\FV@Space {%
            \nobreak\hskip\z@ plus\fontdimen3\font minus\fontdimen4\font
            \discretionary{\copy\Wrappedvisiblespacebox}{\Wrappedafterbreak}
            {\kern\fontdimen2\font}%
        }%

        \Wrappedbreaksatspecials

        \OriginalVerbatim[#1,codes*=\Wrappedbreaksatpunct]%

\begin{document}

    \section{06 --- Métodos de Machine Learning para Construcción de
Portafolio}\label{muxe9todos-de-machine-learning-para-construcciuxf3n-de-portafolio}

\begin{quote}
\textbf{Proyecto}: Optimización de Portafolios con ML --- Tecnológico de
Monterrey, AD2026\\
\textbf{Etapa}: 06 / 09 --- Supervised Learning para Portafolios\\
\textbf{Datos}: \texttt{data/processed/features\_clean.parquet}
(universo final procesado)
\end{quote}

\subsection{Objetivo}\label{objetivo}

Construir portafolios de inversión usando métodos supervisados de ML que
\textbf{predigan retornos futuros} y \textbf{transformen esas
predicciones en pesos del portafolio}, superando el baseline Markowitz.

\subsection{Pipeline general}\label{pipeline-general}

\begin{verbatim}
features_clean.parquet
      │
      ├── 1. EDA rápido de features disponibles
      ├── 2. Ingeniería de target (retorno forward 21d)
      ├── 3. División Walk-Forward (CV temporal)
      │
      ├── MODELOS DE PREDICCIÓN DE RETORNOS:
      │     ├── 4a. Ridge / Lasso Regression
      │     ├── 4b. Random Forest Regressor
      │     └── 4c. XGBoost Regressor
      │
      ├── 5. Señales de ranking → pesos del portafolio (long-only)
      ├── 6. Backtesting mensual y métricas OOS
      └── 7. Comparativa vs Markowitz baseline
\end{verbatim}

    \PY{n}{features}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{names} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Ticker}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}
    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Estructura: MultiIndex (Date, Ticker)}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Columna de ticker identificada}

\subsection{2. Ingeniería del Target y Selección de
Features}\label{ingenieruxeda-del-target-y-selecciuxf3n-de-features}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{ }{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ── Cargar precios para construir retornos forward ───────────────────────────}
\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}raw} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{read\PYZus{}csv}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{CSV\PYZus{}PATH}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{business\PYZus{}days} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bdate\PYZus{}range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{start}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Timestamp}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{FECHA\PYZus{}INICIO}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{periods}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}raw}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}raw}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{business\PYZus{}days}
\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}raw}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{name} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}
\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}raw} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}raw}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{drop}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{columns}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{promedio}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{errors}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ignore}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n}{use\PYZus{}tickers} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ALL\PYZus{}TICKERS} \PY{o}{+} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{BENCHMARK}\PY{p}{]}
\PY{n}{available}   \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{t} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{t} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{use\PYZus{}tickers} \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{t} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}raw}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{columns}\PY{p}{]}
\PY{n}{prices}      \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}raw}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{available}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{copy}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n}{log\PYZus{}ret}     \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{log}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{prices} \PY{o}{/} \PY{n}{prices}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shift}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dropna}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{how}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{all}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Retorno forward de HORIZON días}
\PY{n}{fwd\PYZus{}ret} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{log\PYZus{}ret}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{ALL\PYZus{}TICKERS}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{rolling}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{HORIZON}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shift}\PY{p}{(}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{n}{HORIZON}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df\PYZus{}raw} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{concat}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{frames}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ignore\PYZus{}index}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df\PYZus{}raw}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}datetime}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df\PYZus{}raw}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df\PYZus{}raw}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{set\PYZus{}index}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Ticker}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dropna}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{subset}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{target\PYZus{}fwd}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{FEATURE\PYZus{}COLS} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{c} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{c} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{columns} \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{c} \PY{o+ow}{not} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{target\PYZus{}fwd}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{]}

    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Dataset ML (construido): }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Features: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{FEATURE\PYZus{}COLS}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{FEATURE\PYZus{}COLS}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}n}\PY{l+s+s1}{Sample del dataset ML:}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{FEATURE\PYZus{}COLS}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{l+m+mi}{6}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{+} \PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{target\PYZus{}fwd}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{head}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{5}\PY{p}{)}

\subsection{3. Walk-Forward Cross-Validation
Setup}\label{walk-forward-cross-validation-setup}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{ }{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{get\PYZus{}monthly\PYZus{}dates}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}index}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{start}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{None}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{end}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{None}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{freq}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ME}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Genera fechas de rebalanceo mensuales dentro del rango de datos.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Retorna lista de Timestamps (ultimo dia habil de cada mes).}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{n}{dates} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}index}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{get\PYZus{}level\PYZus{}values}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{unique}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sort\PYZus{}values}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{start}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{dates} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{dates}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{dates} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Timestamp}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{start}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{]}
    \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{end}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{dates} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{dates}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{dates} \PY{o}{\PYZlt{}}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Timestamp}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{end}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{]}
    \PY{n}{s} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Series}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dates}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{index}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{dates}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{DatetimeIndex}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{s}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dates}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}period}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{M}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{max}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{values}\PY{p}{)}


\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{walk\PYZus{}forward\PYZus{}predict}\PY{p}{(}
    \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{feature\PYZus{}cols}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{model}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{train\PYZus{}end\PYZus{}initial} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{2019\PYZhy{}12\PYZhy{}31}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{oos\PYZus{}start}         \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{2020\PYZhy{}01\PYZhy{}01}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{min\PYZus{}train\PYZus{}months}  \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{24}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{rebalance\PYZus{}freq}    \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ME}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,}
\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Walk\PYZhy{}Forward: en cada fecha de rebalanceo:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{     1. Entrena con todos los datos hasta esa fecha (ventana expandida)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{     2. Predice el retorno forward del mes siguiente}
\PY{l+s+sd}{     3. Guarda predicciones y rankings}

\PY{l+s+sd}{    Returns:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        predictions\PYZus{}df : DataFrame con (Date, Ticker, y\PYZus{}pred, y\PYZus{}true, rank)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{n}{all\PYZus{}dates} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{get\PYZus{}level\PYZus{}values}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{unique}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sort\PYZus{}values}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{oos\PYZus{}dates} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{all\PYZus{}dates}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{all\PYZus{}dates} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Timestamp}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{oos\PYZus{}start}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{]}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Fechas de rebalanceo (ultimo dia bursatil de cada mes)}
    \PY{n}{s\PYZus{}dates}   \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Series}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{oos\PYZus{}dates}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{index}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{oos\PYZus{}dates}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{reb\PYZus{}dates} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{DatetimeIndex}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{s\PYZus{}dates}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{oos\PYZus{}dates}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}period}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{M}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{max}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{values}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{predictions} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}

    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{reb\PYZus{}date} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{reb\PYZus{}dates}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Datos disponibles hasta este punto (sin data leakage)}
        \PY{n}{cutoff} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{reb\PYZus{}date}
        \PY{n}{train\PYZus{}mask} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{get\PYZus{}level\PYZus{}values}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{\PYZlt{}}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{cutoff}
        \PY{n}{test\PYZus{}mask}  \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{get\PYZus{}level\PYZus{}values}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{==} \PY{n}{cutoff}

        \PY{n}{X\PYZus{}train} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{loc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}mask}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{feature\PYZus{}cols}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dropna}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}train} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{loc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}mask}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{target\PYZus{}fwd}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dropna}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Interseccion de indices validos}
        \PY{n}{idx\PYZus{}valid} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{X\PYZus{}train}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{intersection}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{y\PYZus{}train}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{k}{if} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{idx\PYZus{}valid}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{\PYZlt{}} \PY{n}{min\PYZus{}train\PYZus{}months} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{20}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{k}{continue}

        \PY{n}{X\PYZus{}tr} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{X\PYZus{}train}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{loc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{idx\PYZus{}valid}\PY{p}{]}
        \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}tr} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}train}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{loc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{idx\PYZus{}valid}\PY{p}{]}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Test: prediccion para el siguiente periodo}
        \PY{n}{X\PYZus{}test} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{loc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}mask}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{feature\PYZus{}cols}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dropna}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}test} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ml\PYZus{}df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{loc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}mask}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{target\PYZus{}fwd}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dropna}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{idx\PYZus{}test} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{X\PYZus{}test}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{intersection}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{y\PYZus{}test}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{k}{if} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{idx\PYZus{}test}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{k}{continue}

        \PY{k}{try}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fit}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X\PYZus{}tr}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{values}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}tr}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{values}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}pred} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{predict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X\PYZus{}test}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{loc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{idx\PYZus{}test}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{values}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{k}{except} \PY{n+ne}{Exception} \PY{k}{as} \PY{n}{e}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{  Error en }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{reb\PYZus{}date}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{e}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{k}{continue}

        \PY{n}{tickers\PYZus{}test} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{idx\PYZus{}test}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{get\PYZus{}level\PYZus{}values}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Ticker}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{idx}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ticker}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{enumerate}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{zip}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{idx\PYZus{}test}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{tickers\PYZus{}test}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{predictions}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{\PYZob{}}
                \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}  \PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{reb\PYZus{}date}\PY{p}{,}
                \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Ticker}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{ticker}\PY{p}{,}
                \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{y\PYZus{}pred}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}pred}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,}
                \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{y\PYZus{}true}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}test}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{loc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{idx}\PY{p}{]} \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{idx} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}test}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index} \PY{k}{else} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{nan}\PY{p}{,}
            \PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{pred\PYZus{}df} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{DataFrame}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{predictions}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{if} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{pred\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{pred\PYZus{}df}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Ranking de predicciones (rank 1 = mejor prediccion)}
    \PY{n}{pred\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{rank}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pred\PYZus{}df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{y\PYZus{}pred}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{rank}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{ascending}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{False}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{method}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{first}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{astype}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{int}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{pred\PYZus{}df}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Funciones de walk\PYZhy{}forward definidas}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{  OOS a partir de: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{OOS\PYZus{}START}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{  Horizonte forward: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{HORIZON}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ dias}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{  Top\PYZhy{}K en portafolio: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{TOP\PYZus{}K}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{ }{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{predictions\PYZus{}to\PYZus{}weights}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{pred\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{top\PYZus{}k}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{10}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{scheme}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{uniform}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Convierte predicciones de ranking a pesos del portafolio.}

\PY{l+s+sd}{    scheme:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{      \PYZsq{}uniform\PYZsq{}  → 1/K para los top\PYZhy{}K}
\PY{l+s+sd}{      \PYZsq{}score\PYZsq{}    → pesos proporcionales al score predicho (clip a 0)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{      \PYZsq{}rank\PYZsq{}     → pesos proporcionales inversos al rank (top=mayor peso)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{n}{weights\PYZus{}list} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{date}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{grp} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{pred\PYZus{}df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{top} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{grp}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{nsmallest}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{top\PYZus{}k}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{rank}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{copy}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{scheme} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{uniform}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{top}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{weight}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mf}{1.0} \PY{o}{/} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{top}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{k}{elif} \PY{n}{scheme} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{score}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{scores} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{top}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{y\PYZus{}pred}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{clip}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{lower}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{total}  \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{scores}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{top}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{weight}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{scores} \PY{o}{/} \PY{n}{total} \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{total} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}} \PY{l+m+mi}{0} \PY{k}{else} \PY{l+m+mi}{1} \PY{o}{/} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{top}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{k}{elif} \PY{n}{scheme} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{rank}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{inv\PYZus{}rank} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mf}{1.0} \PY{o}{/} \PY{n}{top}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{rank}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}
            \PY{n}{top}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{weight}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{inv\PYZus{}rank} \PY{o}{/} \PY{n}{inv\PYZus{}rank}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{top}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{date}
        \PY{n}{weights\PYZus{}list}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{top}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Ticker}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{weight}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{concat}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{weights\PYZus{}list}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ignore\PYZus{}index}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}


\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{backtest\PYZus{}portfolio}\PY{p}{(}
    \PY{n}{weights\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{log\PYZus{}ret}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{benchmark\PYZus{}ticker}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{BENCHMARK}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{horizon}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{21}\PY{p}{,}
\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Backtesting mensual: aplica pesos del mes t a los retornos del mes t+1.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Retorna serie de retornos diarios del portafolio.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{n}{portfolio\PYZus{}rets} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
    \PY{n}{dates} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{weights\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{unique}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{dates} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{sorted}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dates}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{reb\PYZus{}date} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{enumerate}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dates}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{next\PYZus{}reb} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{dates}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{i}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]} \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{i} \PY{o}{+} \PY{l+m+mi}{1} \PY{o}{\PYZlt{}} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dates}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{else} \PY{k+kc}{None}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Pesos en este período}
        \PY{n}{w\PYZus{}row} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{weights\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{weights\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Date}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{==} \PY{n}{reb\PYZus{}date}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{set\PYZus{}index}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Ticker}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{weight}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Período de aplicación: desde reb\PYZus{}date hasta next\PYZus{}reb}
        \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{next\PYZus{}reb} \PY{o+ow}{is} \PY{o+ow}{not} \PY{k+kc}{None}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{mask} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{log\PYZus{}ret}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}} \PY{n}{reb\PYZus{}date}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{\PYZam{}} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{log\PYZus{}ret}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index} \PY{o}{\PYZlt{}}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{next\PYZus{}reb}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{k}{else}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{mask} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{log\PYZus{}ret}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}} \PY{n}{reb\PYZus{}date}

        \PY{n}{ret\PYZus{}period} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{log\PYZus{}ret}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{mask}\PY{p}{]}
        \PY{k}{if} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{ret\PYZus{}period}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{k}{continue}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Filtrar tickers disponibles}
        \PY{n}{tickers} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{t} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{t} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{w\PYZus{}row}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index} \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{t} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{ret\PYZus{}period}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{columns}\PY{p}{]}
        \PY{k}{if} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{tickers}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{k}{continue}

        \PY{n}{w} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{w\PYZus{}row}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{tickers}\PY{p}{]}
        \PY{n}{w} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{w} \PY{o}{/} \PY{n}{w}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{r\PYZus{}period} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ret\PYZus{}period}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{tickers}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dot}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{w}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{portfolio\PYZus{}rets}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{r\PYZus{}period}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{if} \PY{o+ow}{not} \PY{n}{portfolio\PYZus{}rets}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Series}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dtype}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n+nb}{float}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{concat}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{portfolio\PYZus{}rets}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sort\PYZus{}index}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Funciones de backtesting definidas}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Predicciones Ridge: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{pred\PYZus{}ridge}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ filas | }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{pred\PYZus{}ridge}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Date}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{nunique}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ fechas}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\subsection{10. Conclusiones}\label{conclusiones}

\subsubsection{Hallazgos}\label{hallazgos}

{
\begin{longtable}[]{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 4\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2759}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 4\tabcolsep) * \real{0.3793}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 4\tabcolsep) * \real{0.3448}}@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
\begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Modelo
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Fortaleza
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Debilidad
\end{minipage} \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
\textbf{Ridge} & Estable, baja varianza, buena base & Limitado a
relaciones lineales \\
\textbf{Lasso} & Seleccion de features, interpretable & Puede ser
inestable con multicolinealidad \\
\textbf{Random Forest} & Captura no-linealidades, robusto & Lento para
refit frecuente, overfitting con NAs \\
\textbf{XGBoost} & Alto poder predictivo, maneja outliers & Riesgo de
overfitting en ventanas cortas \\
\end{longtable}
}

\subsubsection{Metricas clave}\label{metricas-clave}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{IC \textgreater{} 0.03} indica senales estadisticamente utiles
  para ranking
\item
  \textbf{Sharpe \textgreater{} SPY} valida la adicion de valor del
  modelo ML
\item
  \textbf{Max Drawdown \textless{} Markowitz GMV} es el objetivo de
  proteccion ante shocks
\end{itemize}
